# City plan content

What is on each city's "The Plan" (the same content as src/data/city-plans.json), with where every photo came from. Cities not listed here still have their old plan. The article URLs are in city-plan-urls.md.

To replace a photo: overwrite the file named in the table, in public/images/plans/<city>/ (same name, no code change).

"Map pin" is the place the card opens on the page's map; "none" means the card has no View on the map link.

## New York

### Day 1: Upper East Side

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Jog the reservoir | Jacqueline Kennedy Onassis Reservoir | Run the 1.58-mile loop around the Jackie O Reservoir like a local, pausing at the overlooks for city views and resident herons. | Breakfast at Breads Bakery: chocolate babka and rugelach to go. | day1-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69c44602bdbd0ceb726737cb/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | Get lost at The Frick | The Frick Collection | Head for the newly reopened Frick Collection, a Gilded Age mansion strong in Old Masters and European sculpture. Just over an hour. | Lunch at Café Sabarsky: goulash soup or schnitzel, then Sacher torte. | day1-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/67fee47488019495ea743e0a/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | Check out a jazz show | The Jazz Club at Aman New York | Come for the jazz, from Grammy-winning bassists to powerhouse vocalists, and stay for the chic atmosphere and DJ set. | Dinner at Chez FiFi: French fare in a mahogany-paneled townhouse; book early. | day1-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69c44c727c09757a7eddf7de/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

### Day 2: West Village

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Stroll the Village | The West Village | Walking the West Village on a sunny spring day is pure ambrosia. Lose yourself on Commerce Street and visit Stonewall. | Breakfast at Café Cluny: grapefruit brûlée and a French omelet. | day2-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69c44d994ddbd6123b97fa46/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | Three Lives & Company | Three Lives & Company | Spend an hour in this indie bookstore, a neighbourhood touchstone with a great travel-book section. Left Bank Books is nearby. | Lunch at Moustache Pitza: paper-thin pita with dips and grilled meats. | day2-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69c44fbd6cb23da49552c4d5/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | Cocktails and comedy | Comedy Cellar | Start with a cocktail at Little Branch, a 1920s-style bar behind an unmarked door, then catch the Comedy Cellar or the Village Vanguard. | Late-night: a folded slice at Joe's Pizza, the real nightcap. | day2-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/611fc78c7d5f8669f278de92/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

### Day 3: Carroll Gardens

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Souvenir shopping | Books Are Magic | Walk up Court and Smith Streets for indie fashion and cafés. Emma Straub's Books Are Magic could take up the whole morning. | Breakfast at Court Street Grocers: eggs and cheddar on ciabatta. | day3-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69c454907c09757a7eddf860/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | A flick in Cobble Hill | Cobble Hill Cinemas | Catch an impromptu movie at this neighbourhood joint, which opened in the 1920s as the Lido. Bring a spicy hot chocolate from next door. | Lunch: Caputo's cold cuts and Monteleone cannoli for Carroll Park. | day3-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69c57d3c6d0402da85a71b85/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | Dancing at Public Records | Public Records | Technically in Gowanus, this repurposed warehouse holds a hi-fi listening bar, a vegan café by day and a nightclub. | Dinner at Frankies Spuntino: the crowd-favourite ravioli in the garden. | day3-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69c57e0936328bf3af74421a/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

## Miami

### Day 1: South Miami

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Yoga (or Pilates) | none | Anchor the trip with a workout: Wild Thing's heated yoga class is always a push in the best way. | Breakfast at Facade Bakery: cornflake matcha, olive oil cake and perfectly executed egg dishes. | day1-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/6aba9d4a56fc04ca26f09984/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | A walk around Coconut Grove | Mr. C Miami – Coconut Grove | Spend the afternoon on a walk around Coconut Grove. | Lunch at Krüs Kitchen: farm-to-table fare upstairs from Michelin-starred Los Félix, by the same chef. | day1-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69daa8805fe732c2fe9c37d1/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | Nightcaps with a side of vinyl | none | A vinyl lounge just upstairs from Fooq's, with low ceilings, Persian rugs, brass accents and only a handful of tables. | Dinner at Fooq's: shareable plates with Persian and Mediterranean influences. | day1-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69dd1a43b6740a144dba149a/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

### Day 2: Biscayne and Beyond

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Sweat session, then the beach | none | Heated Pilates at Tondo Room, then straight to the wide, clean beach at The Ritz-Carlton, Key Biscayne. | Breakfast at Flour & Weirdoughs: croissants that shatter when you bite into them. | day2-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69dab22ed70744acec2fcf9e/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | The Rubell Museum | Rubell Museum | One of the largest private collections of contemporary art in North America, collected early in the artists' careers. | Lunch at Gramps Getaway: Florida Keys shack energy and cold drinks in plastic cups. | day2-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/5f9c5d0545d364128d673962/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | Live music at Zey Zey | none | An open-air cultural space, part live music venue, part food hall, with a lineup of artists who usually skip Miami. | Dinner at Sunny's Steakhouse: steak, pasta and house rolls under a massive tree. | day2-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69dabc5a4e667be65ef917a1/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## Barcelona

### Day 1: Classic eats

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | A Day in Sants food tour | Sants | Culinary Backstreets' Feasting Off the Grid tour: intimate, run by food writers who know the restaurants personally. | Breakfast, morning and lunch all come on the tour, which eats up most of the day. | day1-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69f0c8580098fd294c354234/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | Rest before dinner | none | The tour eats up most of the day. A nap afterwards is highly recommended before dinner at Lluritu. | Lunch is on the tour too, so come hungry. | day1-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/5a8c4b4d54350142364a3dde/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | A flamenco performance | none | End the night with a flamenco performance. | Dinner at Lluritu: seafood cooked brilliantly; get the huevos rotos con gambas al ajillo. | day1-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69f0c5f61228ec0a190f6c1a/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

### Day 2: All about the arts

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Take in some art | none | Set aside a couple of hours for VASTO Gallery, the most beautiful in Barcelona, with incredible openings and events. | Breakfast at ETL: Mexican fare modernised beautifully; everything with corn is divine. | day2-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69f8060a6c058ee67154e5d9/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | More art at La Fabra | none | Explore more contemporary, less mainstream artistic voices at La Fabra, whose current exhibition is potent and relevant. | Lunch at Achaar Bar: one of the most delicious dahls of your life. | day2-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69e11f972a6f91b7d56572b2/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | Live music at La Paloma | La Paloma | On a Sunday from 6 to 9 p.m., join all ages dancing to Latin music, especially salsa, in a century-old ballroom. | Dinner at Entrepreneur: classic Catalan, with historic architecture and green velvet seats. | day2-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69f8078c6d7b68d36ae569d7/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

### Day 3: The city's great outdoors

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | A dose of culture | none | Start the day with a dose of culture. | Breakfast at Hotel Brummel: baked goods on the inner patio where creatives gather. | day3-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69e1183e5eea224e55105055/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

Easy to book as a group or solo, or go by paddleboard. | Lunch at Osteria Borbotta: Tuscan homemade pasta stretched right in front of you. | day3-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69f80a8491438b1a8bbcd6c8/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | A photo walk through Kastellet | Kastellet (the Citadel) | One of the most beautiful and peaceful places in town, especially at the start and end of the day when there are fewer tourists. | Breakfast at Atelier September: the open-face avocado on rye and the omelette. | day1-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69fa27d43d333034f79f05c5/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | A dose of art and design | none | Spend the afternoon with a dose of art and design. | Lunch at Bottega Barlie: sourdough with olive oil, the omelette and everything with fish. | day1-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69f8a87ceed6e3c5719e70fe/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | Tivoli time | Tivoli Gardens | Even more magical when the sun and crowds go away: at night there's breathing room to take everything in at your own pace. | Dinner at Vinoria Lilo: the three-course menu, with natural wine and candlelight. | day1-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/5bfdad5a2bc2f13ebecd5af3/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Dive into Inner City | none | Dive into the Inner City. | Breakfast at Café Beau Marché: Parisian-inspired fare in a photogenic backyard. | day2-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69fe0d85a7c4637259501ce7/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | A quick dip at Nordhavn | none | Modern and industrial. Do as the Danes do: jump in the water, then heat up in the sauna at Sauna House. | Lunch at Louise Roe Bar and Gallery: eat off the brand's own plates and glasses. | day2-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69fe0dd5d7e8429a878ce0e0/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Louisiana Museum of Modern Art | Louisiana Museum of Modern Art (Humlebæk) | Take the train to Humlebæk and walk to this modernistic architectural masterpiece, guaranteed to leave an impression. | Breakfast at Wulff & Konstali: pastries near the shore at Klampenborg. | day3-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/5bfc8ae61f85c352ef8d2329/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | Unwind in King's Garden | none | Back in the city, take a break in one of the author's favourite parks, or visit the Palm House if it rains. | Lunch at the Louisiana Café: ocean views outside, a crackling fireplace inside. | day3-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69fa288c3d333034f79f05ce/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Morning | A walk along the River Tagus | Pousada de Lisboa, Praça do Comércio | Avoid the afternoon crowds and stroll the river early, from Praça do Comércio as far as your legs will take you. | Breakfast at Heim Café: the savory French toast with green sauce. | day1-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69d92e114665046e0e75e3a7/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Evening | A sunset river cruise | none | Seeing Lisbon from the water is special, especially on a warm summer evening. |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Port tasting | none | Charming sommeliers talk you through tawny, ruby and white ports until you're debating how many bottles fit in your suitcase. | Breakfast at Neighbourhood: breakfast burritos worth the line. | day2-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69e668039b37d6b601a5f0dd/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |

| Morning | Belém Tower views | Belém Tower (Torre de Belém / Torre de São Vicente) | A 16th-century fortress and UNESCO site, fascinating up close. |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | A wander through Hyde Park | Mandarin Oriental Hyde Park, London | One of London's largest green spaces, perfect in any season and a jewel box of browns, reds and golds in autumn. | Breakfast at Mount Street Restaurant: British classics among works by Warhol and Matisse. | day1-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/58cac9c2fa5cc7498d174de5/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Morning | Walk through Los Rosales | Templo de Debod | Stop at the Temple of Debod, an Egyptian temple from the 2nd century BC, and the Royal Palace, with unbeatable views. | Breakfast at Alma Nomad Bakery: a ham and cheese croissant that becomes something delicious. | day1-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69d425813cda3c5996f16a9a/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Morning | Embrace the chaos at El Rastro | El Rastro | Visit the antique shops on Saturdays, when crowds are more manageable, then stop at Casa Amadeo for snails. | Breakfast at La Duquesita: the best chocolate palmeras in the world; show up early. | day2-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/54f8b74d560f0bf2218ec763/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

### Day 3: A hearty dose of culture

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Morning | Museo Thyssen-Bornemisza | Thyssen-Bornemisza Museum | Both temporary exhibitions and the collection make it one of the best museums in the world. Don't forget the shop. | Breakfast at Estela Hojaldre: the best puff pastry in Madrid. | day3-morning.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/5ced71e2ad20a61eb31dac14/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Afternoon | Go gallery hopping | Carabanchel | Choose Huertas or Lavapiés, or head to Carabanchel, the new art district, for emerging artists and galleries. | Lunch at El Jardín del Ritz: authentic Valencian paella and addictive chicken skewers. | day3-afternoon.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/69d86ddf80e5ebba861b7f9f/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |
| Evening | Experience Spanish Broadway | Teatro Real (Royal Opera House) | Madrid is said to be the Spanish Broadway, with countless musicals. For classics, try the Teatro Español. | Dinner at Corral de la Morería: the best sherry cellar, a Michelin-starred menu and flamenco. | day3-evening.jpg | https://media.cntraveler.com/photos/5ce2f53a766205d8eded140e/master/w_1200,c_limit/x.jpg |

| Slot | Title | Map pin | Body | Food | Photo file | Photo original |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
